### Original Issue

Source: [https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/140404](https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/140404)

````text
# Bug report

### Bug description:

LeakSanitizer detects memory leaks when running `test_import` with the `_testsinglephase` module loaded in a subinterpreter.

### How to Reproduce

**Build:**
```bash
CC=clang CXX=clang++ ./configure --disable-optimizations --with-pydebug --with-address-sanitizer && make -j$(nproc)
```

**Run:**
```bash
./python -X dev -X showrefcount -m test test_import -j0
```

### Leak Summary

```
==643791==ERROR: LeakSanitizer: detected memory leaks
SUMMARY: AddressSanitizer: 6709 byte(s) leaked in 45 allocation(s).
```

### Code Locations

The leaks originate from:

**Subinterpreter execution:**
- https://github.com/python/cpython/blob/e8e0f411bae1dde634fb152a81e1db1ad1cd2eaa/Modules/_testcapimodule.c#L1223
  (`run_in_subinterp`)

**Module initialization:**
- https://github.com/python/cpython/blob/e8e0f411bae1dde634fb152a81e1db1ad1cd2eaa/Modules/_testsinglephase.c#L516
  (`init__testsinglephase_basic`)
- https://github.com/python/cpython/blob/e8e0f411bae1dde634fb152a81e1db1ad1cd2eaa/Modules/_testsinglephase.c#L303
  (`init_state` with `PyErr_NewException`)

**Import machinery:**
- https://github.com/python/cpython/blob/e8e0f411bae1dde634fb152a81e1db1ad1cd2eaa/Python/importdl.c#L429
  (`_PyImport_RunModInitFunc`)

### Leaked Objects

- Type objects (1016 bytes × 2): via `type_new` → `PyErr_NewException`
- Dictionary keys (424 bytes × 2): via `new_keys_object` → `PyDict_SetItemString`
- PyCMethod objects (384 bytes × 2 groups): via `PyModule_AddFunctions`
- Additional: Unicode strings, tuples, dictionaries

### Full Output

See attached `.log` files for complete LeakSanitizer output for each version.

[312.log](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/23016702/312.log)
[313.log](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/23016700/313.log)
[314.log](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/23016703/314.log)
[main.log](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/23016701/main.log)

### CPython versions tested on:

3.12, 3.13, 3.14, 3.15, CPython main branch

### Operating systems tested on:

Linux
````

### Comment 1 — ZeroIntensity, 2025-10-21T15:06:59Z

Source: [https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/140404#issuecomment-3427168845](https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/140404#issuecomment-3427168845)

```text
I think this is fundamentally an unsolvable problem with single-phase initialization. Single-phase doesn't provide a way to clean up process-global state, so things leak. We added multi-phase to fix this problem.
```
